```
     1→// Package settings provides configuration loading for Entire.
     2→// This package is separate from cli to allow strategy package to import it
     3→// without creating an import cycle (cli imports strategy).
     4→package settings
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"bytes"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→const (
    18→	// EntireSettingsFile is the path to the Entire settings file
    19→	EntireSettingsFile = ".entire/settings.json"
    20→	// EntireSettingsLocalFile is the path to the local settings override file (not committed)
    21→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// EntireSettings represents the .entire/settings.json configuration
    25→type EntireSettings struct {
    26→
    27→	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active. When false, CLI commands
    28→	// show a disabled message and hooks exit silently. Defaults to true.
    29→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    30→
    31→	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
    32→	// This is used for development when the binary is not installed
    33→	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    34→
    35→	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error).
    36→	// Can be overridden by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    37→	// Defaults to "info".
    38→	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    39→
    40→	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
    41→	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    42→
    43→	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics.
    44→	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
    45→	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
    46→}
    47→
    48→// Load loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    49→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    50→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    51→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    52→func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
    53→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    54→	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
    57→	}
    58→	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Load base settings
    64→	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Apply local overrides if they exist
    70→	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    73→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
    74→		}
    75→		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
    76→	} else {
    77→		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
    78→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
    79→		}
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	return settings, nil
    83→}
    84→
    85→// LoadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides.
    86→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    87→// Use this when you need to display individual settings files separately.
    88→func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
    89→	return loadFromFile(filePath)
    90→}
    91→
    92→// loadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path.
    93→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    94→func loadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
    95→	settings := &EntireSettings{
    96→		Enabled: true, // Default to enabled
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // path is from caller
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   102→			return settings, nil
   103→		}
   104→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w", err)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   108→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   109→	if err := dec.Decode(settings); err != nil {
   110→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing settings file: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	return settings, nil
   114→}
   115→
   116→// mergeJSON merges JSON data into existing settings.
   117→// Only non-zero values from the JSON override existing settings.
   118→func mergeJSON(settings *EntireSettings, data []byte) error {
   119→	// First, validate that there are no unknown keys using strict decoding
   120→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   121→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   122→	var temp EntireSettings
   123→	if err := dec.Decode(&temp); err != nil {
   124→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Parse into a map to check which fields are present
   128→	var raw map[string]json.RawMessage
   129→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   130→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Override enabled if present
   134→	if enabledRaw, ok := raw["enabled"]; ok {
   135→		var e bool
   136→		if err := json.Unmarshal(enabledRaw, &e); err != nil {
   137→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing enabled field: %w", err)
   138→		}
   139→		settings.Enabled = e
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// Override local_dev if present
   143→	if localDevRaw, ok := raw["local_dev"]; ok {
   144→		var ld bool
   145→		if err := json.Unmarshal(localDevRaw, &ld); err != nil {
   146→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing local_dev field: %w", err)
   147→		}
   148→		settings.LocalDev = ld
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	// Override log_level if present and non-empty
   152→	if logLevelRaw, ok := raw["log_level"]; ok {
   153→		var ll string
   154→		if err := json.Unmarshal(logLevelRaw, &ll); err != nil {
   155→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing log_level field: %w", err)
   156→		}
   157→		if ll != "" {
   158→			settings.LogLevel = ll
   159→		}
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Merge strategy_options if present
   163→	if optionsRaw, ok := raw["strategy_options"]; ok {
   164→		var opts map[string]any
   165→		if err := json.Unmarshal(optionsRaw, &opts); err != nil {
   166→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy_options field: %w", err)
   167→		}
   168→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   169→			settings.StrategyOptions = opts
   170→		} else {
   171→			for k, v := range opts {
   172→				settings.StrategyOptions[k] = v
   173→			}
   174→		}
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Override telemetry if present
   178→	if telemetryRaw, ok := raw["telemetry"]; ok {
   179→		var t bool
   180→		if err := json.Unmarshal(telemetryRaw, &t); err != nil {
   181→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing telemetry field: %w", err)
   182→		}
   183→		settings.Telemetry = &t
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	return nil
   187→}
   188→
   189→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in settings.
   190→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   191→func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   192→	settings, err := Load()
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return false
   195→	}
   196→	return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
   197→}
   198→
   199→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in this settings instance.
   200→func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   201→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   202→		return false
   203→	}
   204→	summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
   205→	if !ok {
   206→		return false
   207→	}
   208→	enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   209→	if !ok {
   210→		return false
   211→	}
   212→	return enabled
   213→}
   214→
   215→// IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
   216→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
   217→func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
   218→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   219→		return false
   220→	}
   221→	val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
   222→	if !exists {
   223→		return false
   224→	}
   225→	if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
   226→		return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
   227→	}
   228→	return false
   229→}
   230→
   231→// Save saves the settings to .entire/settings.json.
   232→func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   233→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
   234→}
   235→
   236→// SaveLocal saves the settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
   237→func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   238→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   239→}
   240→
   241→// saveToFile saves settings to the specified file path.
   242→func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
   243→	// Get absolute path for the file
   244→	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	// Ensure directory exists
   250→	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
   251→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   256→	if err != nil {
   257→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	//nolint:gosec // G306: settings file is config, not secrets; 0o644 is appropriate
   261→	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   262→		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
   263→	}
   264→	return nil
   265→}
   266→
```

```
     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"sort"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[string]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new strategy instance
    15→type Factory func() Strategy
    16→
    17→// Register adds a strategy factory to the registry.
    18→// This is typically called from init() functions in strategy implementations.
    19→func Register(name string, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves a strategy by name.
    26→// Returns an error if the strategy is not registered.
    27→//
    28→
    29→func Get(name string) (Strategy, error) {
    30→	registryMu.RLock()
    31→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    32→
    33→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    34→	if !ok {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	return factory(), nil
    39→}
    40→
    41→// List returns all registered strategy names in sorted order.
    42→func List() []string {
    43→	registryMu.RLock()
    44→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    45→
    46→	names := make([]string, 0, len(registry))
    47→	for name := range registry {
    48→		names = append(names, name)
    49→	}
    50→	sort.Strings(names)
    51→	return names
    52→}
    53→
```

```
    22→// Config path display strings
    23→const (

    25→	configDisplayLocal   = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    26→)
    27→
    28→func newEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
    29→	var localDev bool
    30→	var ignoreUntracked bool
    31→	var useLocalSettings bool
    32→	var useProjectSettings bool
    33→	var agentName string
    34→	var forceHooks bool
    35→	var skipPushSessions bool
    36→	var telemetry bool
    37→
    38→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    39→		Use:   "enable",
    40→		Short: "Enable Entire in current project",
    41→		Long: `Enable Entire with session tracking for your AI agent workflows.
    42→
    43→Uses the manual-commit strategy, which creates session checkpoints without
    44→modifying your active branch.`,
    45→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    46→			// Check if we're in a git repository first - this is a prerequisite error,
    47→			// not a usage error, so we silence Cobra's output and use SilentError
    48→			// to prevent duplicate error output in main.go
    49→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    50→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run 'entire enable' from within a git repository.")
    51→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
    52→			}
    53→
    54→			if err := validateSetupFlags(useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings); err != nil {
    55→				return err
    56→			}
    57→
    58→			// Warn if repo has no commits yet
    59→			if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
    60→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Note: This repository has no commits yet. Entire will be configured, but")
    61→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "session checkpoints won't work until you create your first commit.")
    62→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout())
    63→			}
    64→
    65→			// Non-interactive mode if --agent flag is provided
    66→			if cmd.Flags().Changed("agent") && agentName == "" {
    67→				printMissingAgentError(cmd.ErrOrStderr())
    68→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("missing agent name"))
    69→			}
    70→
    71→			if agentName != "" {
    72→				ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentName(agentName))
    73→				if err != nil {
    74→					printWrongAgentError(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), agentName)
    75→					return NewSilentError(errors.New("wrong agent name"))
    76→				}
    77→				return setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), ag, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
    78→			}
    79→			hasConfigFlags := forceHooks || skipPushSessions || !telemetry || useLocalSettings || useProjectSettings || localDev
    80→			if !hasConfigFlags {
    81→				if fullyEnabled, agentDesc, configPath := isFullyEnabled(); fullyEnabled {
    82→					w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
    83→					fmt.Fprintln(w, "Already enabled. Everything looks good.")
    84→					fmt.Fprintln(w)
    85→					fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Agent: %s\n", agentDesc)
    86→					fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Config: %s\n", configPath)
    87→					return nil
    88→				}
    89→			}
    90→
    91→			// Detect or prompt for agents
    92→			agents, err := detectOrSelectAgent(cmd.OutOrStdout(), nil)
    93→			if err != nil {
    94→				return fmt.Errorf("agent selection failed: %w", err)
    95→			}
    96→
    97→			return runEnableInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), agents, localDev, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
    98→		},
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&localDev, "local-dev", false, "Use go run instead of entire binary for hooks")
   102→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("local-dev") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above
   103→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&ignoreUntracked, "ignore-untracked", false, "Commit all new files without tracking pre-existing untracked files")
   104→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("ignore-untracked") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above
   105→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useLocalSettings, "local", false, "Write settings to .entire/settings.local.json instead of .entire/settings.json")
   106→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Write settings to .entire/settings.json even if it already exists")
   107→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&agentName, "agent", "", "Agent to setup hooks for (e.g., claude-code). Enables non-interactive mode.")

   114→	cmd.SetFlagErrorFunc(func(c *cobra.Command, err error) error {

   119→		}
   120→		return defaultFlagErr(c, err)

   123→	// Add subcommands for automation/testing

   129→func newDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   130→	var useProjectSettings bool
   131→	var uninstall bool
   132→	var force bool
   133→
   134→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   135→		Use:   "disable",
   136→		Short: "Disable Entire in current project",
   137→		Long: `Disable Entire integrations in the current project.
   138→
   139→By default, this command will disable Entire. Hooks will exit silently and commands will
   140→show a disabled message.
   141→
   142→To completely remove Entire integrations from this repository, use --uninstall:
   143→  - .entire/ directory (settings, logs, metadata)
   144→  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)
   145→  - Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
   146→  - Shadow branches (entire/<hash>)
   147→  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)`,
   148→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   149→			if uninstall {
   150→				return runUninstall(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), force)
   151→			}
   152→			return runDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   153→		},
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Update .entire/settings.json instead of .entire/settings.local.json")
   157→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&uninstall, "uninstall", false, "Completely remove Entire from this repository")
   158→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&force, "force", false, "Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall)")
   159→
   160→	return cmd
   161→}
   162→
   163→// isFullyEnabled checks whether Entire is already fully set up.
   164→// Returns whether it's fully enabled, and if so, the agent type display name and config file path.
   165→func isFullyEnabled() (enabled bool, agentDesc string, configPath string) {
   166→	// Check settings exist and Enabled == true
   167→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   168→	if err != nil || !s.Enabled {
   169→		return false, "", ""
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Check any agent hooks installed (not just Claude Code — works with Gemini too)
   173→	installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
   174→	if len(installedAgents) == 0 {
   175→		return false, "", ""
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	// Check git hooks installed
   179→	if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalled() {
   180→		return false, "", ""
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Check .entire directory exists
   184→	if !checkEntireDirExists() {
   185→		return false, "", ""
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Determine agent description from first installed agent
   189→	desc := string(installedAgents[0]) // fallback to agent name
   190→	if ag, err := agent.Get(installedAgents[0]); err == nil {
   191→		desc = string(ag.Type())
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Determine config path - check if local settings exists, otherwise show project settings
   195→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   196→	if err != nil {
   197→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
   198→	}
   199→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   200→	localSettingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDirAbs, "settings.local.json")
   201→	if _, err := os.Stat(localSettingsPath); err == nil {
   202→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	return true, desc, configDisplay
   206→}
   207→
   208→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   209→// agents must be provided by the caller (via detectOrSelectAgent).
   210→// The isFullyEnabled check is handled by the caller before agent detection.
   211→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, agents []agent.Agent, localDev, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   212→	// Setup agent hooks for all selected agents
   213→	for _, ag := range agents {
   214→		if _, err := setupAgentHooks(ag, localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   215→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup %s hooks: %w", ag.Type(), err)
   216→		}
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	// Setup .entire directory
   220→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   221→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   225→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   228→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   229→	}
   230→	// Update the specific fields
   231→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   232→	settings.Enabled = true
   233→
   234→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   235→	if skipPushSessions {
   236→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   237→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   238→		}
   239→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   243→	// First run always creates settings.json (no prompt)
   244→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   247→	}
   248→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   249→
   250→	if showNotification {
   251→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   252→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Helper to save settings to the appropriate file
   256→	saveSettings := func() error {
   257→		if shouldUseLocal {
   258→			return SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings)
   259→		}
   260→		return SaveEntireSettings(settings)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	// Save settings before telemetry prompt so config is persisted even if the user cancels
   264→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   265→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   269→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   270→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   271→	}
   272→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   273→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   274→
   275→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   276→	if shouldUseLocal {
   277→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   278→	}
   279→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   280→
   281→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   282→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   283→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→	// Save again to persist telemetry choice
   287→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   288→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	if err := strategy.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   292→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   296→
   297→	return nil
   298→}
   299→
   300→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   301→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   302→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	settings.Enabled = true
   308→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   313→	return nil
   314→}
   315→
   316→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   317→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	settings.Enabled = false
   323→
   324→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   325→	if useProjectSettings {
   326→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   327→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   328→		}
   329→	} else {
   330→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   331→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   332→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   333→		}
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   337→	return nil
   338→}
   339→
   340→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   341→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   342→
   343→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   344→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   345→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   346→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   347→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   348→	if err != nil {
   349→		// Default to enabled on error
   350→		return false
   351→	}
   352→	if !enabled {
   353→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   354→		return true
   355→	}
   356→	return false
   357→}
   358→
   359→// setupAgentHooks sets up hooks for a given agent.
   360→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   361→func setupAgentHooks(ag agent.Agent, localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) {
   362→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   363→	if !ok {
   364→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", ag.Name())
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   368→	if err != nil {
   369→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install %s hooks: %w", ag.Name(), err)
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	return count, nil
   373→}
   374→
   375→// detectOrSelectAgent tries to auto-detect agents, or prompts the user to select.
   376→// Returns the detected/selected agents and any error.
   377→// When exactly one agent is detected, it is used automatically.
   378→// When multiple agents are detected, the user is prompted to confirm.
   379→// If no agent is detected and no TTY is available, falls back to the default agent.
   380→//
   381→// selectFn overrides the interactive prompt for testing. When nil, the real form is used.
   382→// It receives available agent names and returns the selected names.
   383→func detectOrSelectAgent(w io.Writer, selectFn func(available []string) ([]string, error)) ([]agent.Agent, error) {
   384→	// Try auto-detection first
   385→	detected := agent.DetectAll()
   386→
   387→	switch {
   388→	case len(detected) == 1:
   389→		// Single agent detected — use it directly
   390→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Detected agent: %s\n\n", detected[0].Type())
   391→		return detected, nil
   392→
   393→	case len(detected) > 1:
   394→		// Multiple agents detected — prompt the user to confirm which to enable
   395→		agentTypes := make([]string, 0, len(detected))
   396→		for _, ag := range detected {
   397→			agentTypes = append(agentTypes, string(ag.Type()))
   398→		}
   399→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Detected multiple agents: %s\n", strings.Join(agentTypes, ", "))
   400→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   401→		// Fall through to the interactive multi-select below
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// No agent detected (or multiple detected) — check if we can prompt interactively
   405→	if !canPromptInteractively() {
   406→		if len(detected) > 0 {
   407→			// Multiple agents detected but no TTY — use all of them
   408→			return detected, nil
   409→		}
   410→		// No TTY available (e.g., running in CI or tests) - fall back to default agent
   411→		defaultAgent := agent.Default()
   412→		if defaultAgent == nil {
   413→			return nil, errors.New("no default agent available")
   414→		}
   415→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", defaultAgent.Type())
   416→		return []agent.Agent{defaultAgent}, nil
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	if len(detected) == 0 {
   420→		// Show message only when nothing was detected
   421→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No agent configuration detected (e.g., .claude or .gemini directory).")
   422→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "This is normal - some agents don't require a config directory.")
   423→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	// Build a set of detected agent names for pre-selection
   427→	detectedSet := make(map[agent.AgentName]struct{}, len(detected))
   428→	for _, ag := range detected {
   429→		detectedSet[ag.Name()] = struct{}{}
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	// Build options from registered agents
   433→	agentNames := agent.List()
   434→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(agentNames))
   435→	for _, name := range agentNames {
   436→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   437→		if err != nil {
   438→			continue
   439→		}
   440→		// Only show agents that support hooks
   441→		if _, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); !ok {
   442→			continue
   443→		}
   444→		label := string(ag.Type())
   445→		if name == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   446→			label += " (default)"
   447→		}
   448→		opt := huh.NewOption(label, string(name))
   449→		if _, isDetected := detectedSet[name]; isDetected {
   450→			opt = opt.Selected(true)
   451→		}
   452→		options = append(options, opt)
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	if len(options) == 0 {
   456→		return nil, errors.New("no agents with hook support available")
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	// Collect available agent names for the selector
   460→	availableNames := make([]string, 0, len(options))
   461→	for _, opt := range options {
   462→		availableNames = append(availableNames, opt.Value)
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	var selectedAgentNames []string
   466→	if selectFn != nil {
   467→		var err error
   468→		selectedAgentNames, err = selectFn(availableNames)
   469→		if err != nil {
   470→			return nil, err
   471→		}
   472→	} else {
   473→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   474→			huh.NewGroup(
   475→				huh.NewMultiSelect[string]().
   476→					Title("Which agents are you using?").
   477→					Description("Use space to select, enter to confirm.").
   478→					Options(options...).
   479→					Value(&selectedAgentNames),
   480→			),
   481→		)
   482→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   483→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent selection cancelled: %w", err)
   484→		}
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	if len(selectedAgentNames) == 0 {
   488→		return nil, errors.New("no agents selected")
   489→	}
   490→
   491→	selectedAgents := make([]agent.Agent, 0, len(selectedAgentNames))
   492→	for _, name := range selectedAgentNames {
   493→		selectedAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentName(name))
   494→		if err != nil {
   495→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get selected agent %s: %w", name, err)
   496→		}
   497→		selectedAgents = append(selectedAgents, selectedAgent)
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	agentTypes := make([]string, 0, len(selectedAgents))
   501→	for _, ag := range selectedAgents {
   502→		agentTypes = append(agentTypes, string(ag.Type()))
   503→	}
   504→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nSelected agents: %s\n\n", strings.Join(agentTypes, ", "))
   505→	return selectedAgents, nil
   506→}
   507→
   508→// canPromptInteractively checks if we can show interactive prompts.
   509→// Returns false when running in CI, tests, or other non-interactive environments.
   510→func canPromptInteractively() bool {
   511→	// Check for test environment
   512→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
   513→		return os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") == "1"
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	// Check if /dev/tty is available
   517→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
   518→	if err != nil {
   519→		return false
   520→	}
   521→	_ = tty.Close()
   522→	return true
   523→}
   524→
   525→// printAgentError writes an error message followed by available agents and usage.
   526→func printAgentError(w io.Writer, message string) {
   527→	agents := agent.List()
   528→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s Available agents:\n", message)
   529→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   530→	for _, a := range agents {
   531→		suffix := ""
   532→		if a == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   533→			suffix = "    (default)"
   534→		}
   535→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s%s\n", a, suffix)
   536→	}
   537→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   538→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Usage: entire enable --agent <agent-name>")
   539→}
   540→
   541→// printMissingAgentError writes a helpful error listing available agents.
   542→func printMissingAgentError(w io.Writer) {
   543→	printAgentError(w, "Missing agent name.")
   544→}
   545→
   546→// printWrongAgentError writes a helpful error when an unknown agent name is provided.
   547→func printWrongAgentError(w io.Writer, name string) {
   548→	printAgentError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Unknown agent %q.", name))
   549→}
   550→
   551→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   552→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   553→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(w io.Writer, ag agent.Agent, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   554→	agentName := ag.Name()
   555→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   556→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   557→	if !ok {
   558→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s\n\n", ag.Type())
   562→
   563→	// Install agent hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   564→	installedHooks, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   565→	if err != nil {
   566→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// Setup .entire directory
   570→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   571→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   572→	}
   573→
   574→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   575→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   576→	if err != nil {
   577→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   578→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   579→	}
   580→	settings.Enabled = true
   581→	if localDev {
   582→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   586→	if skipPushSessions {
   587→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   588→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   589→		}
   590→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   591→	}
   592→
   593→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   594→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   595→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   596→		f := false
   597→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   598→	}
   599→
   600→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   601→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   605→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   606→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   607→	}
   608→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   609→
   610→	if installedHooks == 0 {
   611→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   612→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   613→			msg += " (Preview)"
   614→		}
   615→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   616→	} else {
   617→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", installedHooks, ag.Description())
   618→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   619→			msg += " (Preview)"
   620→		}
   621→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplayProject)
   625→
   626→	if err := strategy.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   627→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   631→
   632→	return nil
   633→}
   634→
   635→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   636→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   637→	if useLocal && useProject {
   638→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   639→	}
   640→	return nil
   641→}
   642→
   643→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   644→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   645→// - The --local and --project flags
   646→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   647→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   648→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   649→	if useLocal {
   650→		return true, false
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   654→	if useProject {
   655→		return false, false
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   659→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   660→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   661→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   662→		return true, true
   663→	}
   664→
   665→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   666→	return false, false
   667→}
   668→
   669→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   670→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   671→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   672→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   673→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   674→	if err != nil {
   675→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Check if directory already exists
   679→	created := false
   680→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   681→		created = true
   682→	}
   683→
   684→	// Create .entire directory
   685→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   686→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   687→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   691→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   692→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   693→	}
   694→
   695→	return created, nil
   696→}
   697→
   698→// setupGitHook installs the prepare-commit-msg hook for context trailers.
   699→func setupGitHook() error {
   700→	// Use shared implementation from strategy package
   701→	// The localDev setting is read from settings.json
   702→	_, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(false) // not silent - show output during setup
   703→	if err != nil {
   704→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hook: %w", err)
   705→	}
   706→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(os.Stderr)
   707→	return nil
   708→}
   709→
   710→// newSetupGitHookCmd creates the standalone git-hook setup command
   711→func newSetupGitHookCmd() *cobra.Command {
   712→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   713→		Use:    "git-hook",
   714→		Short:  "Install git hook for session context trailers",
   715→		Hidden: true, // Hidden as it's mainly for testing
   716→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   717→			return setupGitHook()
   718→		},
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	return cmd
   722→}
   723→
   724→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   725→	return &cobra.Command{
   726→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   727→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   728→		Hidden: true,
   729→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   730→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   731→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   732→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   733→			}
   734→			return nil
   735→		},
   736→	}
   737→}
   738→
   739→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   740→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   741→
   742→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   743→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   744→
   745→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   746→// user's current shell.
   747→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   748→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   749→	if err != nil {
   750→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   751→	}
   752→
   753→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   754→	switch {
   755→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   756→		return "Zsh",
   757→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   758→			"autoload -Uz compinit && compinit && source <(entire completion zsh)",
   759→			nil
   760→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   761→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   762→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   763→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   764→		}
   765→		return "Bash",
   766→			bashRC,
   767→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   768→			nil
   769→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   770→		return "Fish",
   771→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   772→			"entire completion fish | source",
   773→			nil
   774→	default:
   775→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   776→	}
   777→}
   778→
   779→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   780→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   781→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   782→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   783→	if err != nil {
   784→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   785→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   786→			return nil
   787→		}
   788→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   789→	}
   790→
   791→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   792→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   793→		return nil
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	var selected string
   797→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   798→		huh.NewGroup(
   799→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   800→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)).
   801→				Options(
   802→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   803→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   804→				).
   805→				Value(&selected),
   806→		),
   807→	)
   808→
   809→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   810→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   811→		return nil
   812→	}
   813→
   814→	if selected != "yes" {
   815→		return nil
   816→	}
   817→
   818→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   819→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   820→	}
   821→
   822→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   823→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   824→
   825→	return nil
   826→}
   827→
   828→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
   829→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
   830→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   831→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   832→	if err != nil {
   833→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
   834→	}
   835→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
   836→}
   837→
   838→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
   839→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   840→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
   841→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
   842→	}
   843→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   844→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
   845→	if err != nil {
   846→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
   847→	}
   848→	defer f.Close()
   849→
   850→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
   851→	if err != nil {
   852→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
   853→	}
   854→	return nil
   855→}
   856→
   857→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
   858→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
   859→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
   860→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
   861→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
   862→	if !telemetryFlag {
   863→		f := false
   864→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   865→		return nil
   866→	}
   867→
   868→	// Skip if already asked
   869→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
   870→		return nil
   871→	}
   872→
   873→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
   874→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   875→		f := false
   876→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   877→		return nil
   878→	}
   879→
   880→	consent := true // Default to Yes
   881→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   882→		huh.NewGroup(
   883→			huh.NewConfirm().
   884→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
   885→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
   886→				Affirmative("Yes").
   887→				Negative("No").
   888→				Value(&consent),
   889→		),
   890→	)
   891→
   892→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   893→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
   894→	}
   895→
   896→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
   897→	return nil
   898→}
   899→
   900→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
   901→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
   902→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   903→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   904→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
   905→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   906→	}
   907→
   908→	// Gather counts for display
   909→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
   910→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
   911→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
   912→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
   913→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
   914→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
   915→
   916→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
   917→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
   918→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
   919→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
   920→		return nil
   921→	}
   922→
   923→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
   924→	if !force {
   925→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
   926→		if entireDirExists {
   927→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
   928→		}
   929→		if gitHooksInstalled {
   930→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   931→		}
   932→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
   933→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
   934→		}
   935→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
   936→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
   937→		}
   938→		switch {
   939→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
   940→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
   941→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
   942→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
   943→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
   944→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
   945→		}
   946→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   947→
   948→		var confirmed bool
   949→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   950→			huh.NewGroup(
   951→				huh.NewConfirm().
   952→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
   953→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
   954→					Negative("Cancel").
   955→					Value(&confirmed),
   956→			),
   957→		)
   958→
   959→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   960→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   961→		}
   962→
   963→		if !confirmed {
   964→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
   965→			return nil
   966→		}
   967→	}
   968→
   969→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
   970→
   971→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
   972→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
   973→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
   974→	}
   975→
   976→	// 2. Remove git hooks
   977→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
   978→	if err != nil {
   979→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
   980→	} else if removed > 0 {
   981→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
   982→	}
   983→
   984→	// 3. Remove session state files
   985→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
   986→	if err != nil {
   987→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
   988→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
   989→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
   990→	}
   991→
   992→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
   993→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   994→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
   995→	} else if entireDirExists {
   996→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
   997→	}
   998→
   999→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1000→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1001→	if err != nil {
  1002→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1003→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1004→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1005→	}
  1006→
  1007→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1008→	return nil
  1009→}
  1010→
  1011→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1012→func countSessionStates() int {
  1013→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1014→	if err != nil {
  1015→		return 0
  1016→	}
  1017→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1018→	if err != nil {
  1019→		return 0
  1020→	}
  1021→	return len(states)
  1022→}
  1023→
  1024→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1025→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1026→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1027→	if err != nil {
  1028→		return 0
  1029→	}
  1030→	return len(branches)
  1031→}

  1033→// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.
  1034→func checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {
  1035→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1036→	if err != nil {
  1037→		return false
  1038→	}
  1039→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1040→	if !ok {
  1041→		return false
  1042→	}
  1043→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1044→}
  1045→
  1046→// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.
  1047→func checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {
  1048→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1049→	if err != nil {
  1050→		return false
  1051→	}
  1052→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1053→	if !ok {

  1055→	}

  1059→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
  1060→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {

  1062→	if err != nil {

  1064→	}

  1068→
  1069→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1070→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1071→	var errs []error
  1072→
  1073→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1074→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1075→	if err == nil {
  1076→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1077→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1078→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1079→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1080→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1081→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1082→			}
  1083→		}
  1084→	}
  1085→
  1086→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1087→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1088→	if err == nil {
  1089→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1090→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1091→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1092→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1093→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1094→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1095→			}
  1096→		}
  1097→	}
  1098→
  1099→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1100→}
  1101→
  1102→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1103→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1104→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1105→	if err != nil {
  1106→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1107→	}
  1108→
  1109→	// Count states before removing
  1110→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1111→	if err != nil {
  1112→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1113→	}
  1114→	count := len(states)
  1115→
  1116→	// Remove the entire directory
  1117→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1118→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1119→	}
  1120→
  1121→	return count, nil
  1122→}
  1123→
  1124→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1125→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1126→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1127→	if err != nil {
  1128→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1129→	}
  1130→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1131→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1132→	}
  1133→	return nil
  1134→}
  1135→
  1136→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1137→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1138→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1139→	if err != nil {
  1140→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list shadow branches: %w", err)
  1141→	}
  1142→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1143→		return 0, nil
  1144→	}
  1145→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1146→	return len(deleted), err
  1147→}
```

```
    24→func newStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {

    26→

    28→		Use:   "status",

    41→func runStatus(w io.Writer, detailed bool) error {
    42→	// Check if we're in a git repository
    43→	if _, repoErr := paths.RepoRoot(); repoErr != nil {
    44→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✕ not a git repository")
    45→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Not being in a git repo is a valid status, not an error
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    49→	settingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    50→	if err != nil {
    51→		settingsPath = EntireSettingsFile
    52→	}
    53→	localSettingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    54→	if err != nil {
    55→		localSettingsPath = EntireSettingsLocalFile
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Check which settings files exist
    59→	_, projectErr := os.Stat(settingsPath)
    60→	if projectErr != nil && !errors.Is(projectErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
    61→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access project settings file: %w", projectErr)
    62→	}
    63→	_, localErr := os.Stat(localSettingsPath)
    64→	if localErr != nil && !errors.Is(localErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access local settings file: %w", localErr)
    66→	}
    67→	projectExists := projectErr == nil
    68→	localExists := localErr == nil
    69→
    70→	if !projectExists && !localExists {
    71→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "○ not set up (run `entire enable` to get started)")
    72→		return nil
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	if detailed {
    76→		return runStatusDetailed(w, settingsPath, localSettingsPath, projectExists, localExists)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Short output: just show the effective/merged state

    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
    83→	}

    85→	fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))

    87→	if settings.Enabled {

    91→	return nil
    92→}

   129→// formatSettingsStatusShort formats a short settings status line.
   130→// Output format: "Enabled (manual-commit)" or "Disabled (manual-commit)"
   131→func formatSettingsStatusShort(settings *EntireSettings) string {
   132→	if settings.Enabled {
   133→		return fmt.Sprintf("Enabled (%s)", strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   134→	}
   135→	return fmt.Sprintf("Disabled (%s)", strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   136→}

   138→// formatSettingsStatus formats a settings status line with source prefix.
   139→// Output format: "Project, enabled (manual-commit)" or "Local, disabled (manual-commit)"
   140→func formatSettingsStatus(prefix string, settings *EntireSettings) string {
   141→	if settings.Enabled {
   142→		return fmt.Sprintf("%s, enabled (%s)", prefix, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   143→	}
   144→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s, disabled (%s)", prefix, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   145→}
   146→
   147→// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.
   148→func timeAgo(t time.Time) string {
   149→	d := time.Since(t)
   150→	switch {
   151→	case d < time.Minute:
   152→		return "just now"
   153→	case d < time.Hour:
   154→		m := int(d.Minutes())
   155→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", m)
   156→	case d < 24*time.Hour:
   157→		h := int(d.Hours())
   158→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", h)
   159→	default:
   160→		days := int(d.Hours() / 24)
   161→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dd ago", days)
   162→	}
   163→}
   164→
   165→// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.
   166→type worktreeGroup struct {
   167→	path     string
   168→	branch   string
   169→	sessions []*session.State
   170→}
   171→
   172→const unknownPlaceholder = "(unknown)"
   173→
   174→// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.
   175→func writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {
   176→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		return
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   182→	if err != nil || len(states) == 0 {
   183→		return
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	// Filter to active sessions only
   187→	var active []*session.State
   188→	for _, s := range states {
   189→		if s.EndedAt == nil {
   190→			active = append(active, s)
   191→		}
   192→	}
   193→	if len(active) == 0 {
   194→		return
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	// Group by worktree path
   198→	groups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)
   199→	for _, s := range active {
   200→		wp := s.WorktreePath
   201→		if wp == "" {
   202→			wp = unknownPlaceholder
   203→		}
   204→		g, ok := groups[wp]
   205→		if !ok {
   206→			g = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}
   207→			groups[wp] = g
   208→		}
   209→		g.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Resolve branch names for each worktree (skip for unknown paths)
   213→	for _, g := range groups {
   214→		if g.path != unknownPlaceholder {
   215→			g.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)
   216→		}
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	// Sort groups: alphabetical by path
   220→	sortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))
   221→	for _, g := range groups {
   222→		sortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)
   223→	}
   224→	sort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {
   225→		return sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path
   226→	})
   227→
   228→	// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)
   229→	for _, g := range sortedGroups {
   230→		sort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
   231→			return g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)
   232→		})
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   236→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Active Sessions:")
   237→	for i, g := range sortedGroups {
   238→		header := g.path
   239→		if g.branch != "" {
   240→			header += " (" + g.branch + ")"
   241→		}
   242→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s\n", header)
   243→
   244→		for _, st := range g.sessions {
   245→			agentLabel := string(st.AgentType)
   246→			if agentLabel == "" {
   247→				agentLabel = unknownPlaceholder
   248→			}
   249→
   250→			shortID := st.SessionID
   251→			if len(shortID) > 7 {
   252→				shortID = shortID[:7]
   253→			}
   254→
   255→			age := "started " + timeAgo(st.StartedAt)
   256→
   257→			// Show "active X ago" when LastInteractionTime differs meaningfully from StartedAt
   258→			activeStr := ""
   259→			if st.LastInteractionTime != nil && st.LastInteractionTime.Sub(st.StartedAt) > time.Minute {
   260→				activeStr = ", active " + timeAgo(*st.LastInteractionTime)
   261→			}
   262→
   263→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "    [%s] %-9s %s%s\n",
   264→				agentLabel, shortID, age, activeStr)
   265→
   266→			// Show first prompt on indented second line
   267→			if st.FirstPrompt != "" {
   268→				prompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(st.FirstPrompt, 60, "...")
   269→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "      \"%s\"\n", prompt)
   270→			}
   271→		}
   272→
   273→		// Blank line between groups, but not after the last one
   274→		if i < len(sortedGroups)-1 {
   275→			fmt.Fprintln(w)
   276→		}
   277→	}
   278→}
   279→
   280→// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.
   281→func resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {
   282→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "-C", worktreePath, "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")
   283→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   284→	if err != nil {
   285→		return ""
   286→	}
   287→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   288→}
   289→
```

```
    10→

    14→
    15→// Package-level aliases to avoid shadowing the settings package with local variables named "settings".
    16→const (
    17→	EntireSettingsFile      = settings.EntireSettingsFile
    18→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile
    19→)
    20→
    21→// EntireSettings is an alias for settings.EntireSettings.
    22→type EntireSettings = settings.EntireSettings
    23→
    24→// LoadEntireSettings loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    25→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    26→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    27→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    28→func LoadEntireSettings() (*settings.EntireSettings, error) {
    29→	s, err := settings.Load()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("loading settings: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→	return s, nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// SaveEntireSettings saves the Entire settings to .entire/settings.json.
    37→func SaveEntireSettings(s *settings.EntireSettings) error {
    38→	if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("saving settings: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// SaveEntireSettingsLocal saves the Entire settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
    45→func SaveEntireSettingsLocal(s *settings.EntireSettings) error {
    46→	if err := settings.SaveLocal(s); err != nil {
    47→		return fmt.Errorf("saving local settings: %w", err)
    48→	}
    49→	return nil
    50→}
    51→
    52→// IsEnabled returns whether Entire is currently enabled.
    53→// Returns true by default if settings cannot be loaded.
    54→func IsEnabled() (bool, error) {
    55→	s, err := settings.Load()
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		return true, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
    58→	}
    59→	return s.Enabled, nil
    60→}
    61→
    62→// GetStrategy returns the configured strategy instance.
    63→// Falls back to default if the configured strategy is not found.
    64→//
    65→
    66→func GetStrategy() strategy.Strategy {
    67→	return strategy.NewManualCommitStrategy()
    68→}
    69→
    70→// GetLogLevel returns the configured log level from settings.
    71→// Returns empty string if not configured (caller should use default).
    72→// Note: ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL env var takes precedence; check it first.
    73→func GetLogLevel() string {
    74→	s, err := settings.Load()
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		return ""
    77→	}
    78→	return s.LogLevel
    79→}
    80→
    81→// GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled returns names of agents that have hooks installed.
    82→func GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled() []agent.AgentName {
    83→	var installed []agent.AgentName
    84→	for _, name := range agent.List() {
    85→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
    86→		if err != nil {
    87→			continue
    88→		}
    89→		if hs, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); ok && hs.AreHooksInstalled() {
    90→			installed = append(installed, name)
    91→		}
    92→	}
    93→	return installed
    94→}
    95→
    96→// JoinAgentNames joins agent names into a comma-separated string.
    97→func JoinAgentNames(names []agent.AgentName) string {
    98→	strs := make([]string, len(names))
    99→	for i, n := range names {
   100→		strs[i] = string(n)
   101→	}
   102→	return strings.Join(strs, ",")
   103→}
   104→
```

```
   328→	// Try to get remote metadata branch tree
   329→	remoteTree, err := strategy.GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   330→	if err != nil {
   331→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Checkpoint '%s' found in commit but session metadata not available\n", checkpointID)
   332→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "The entire/checkpoints/v1 branch may not exist locally or on the remote.\n")

   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Check if the checkpoint exists on the remote
   337→	metadata, err := strategy.ReadCheckpointMetadata(remoteTree, checkpointID.Path())
   338→	if err != nil {
   339→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Checkpoint '%s' found in commit but session metadata not available\n", checkpointID)
   340→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Informational message, not a fatal error
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Metadata exists on remote but not locally - fetch it automatically
   344→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Fetching session metadata from origin...\n")
   345→	if err := FetchMetadataBranch(); err != nil {
   346→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Failed to fetch metadata: %v\n", err)
   347→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "You can try manually: git fetch origin entire/checkpoints/v1:entire/checkpoints/v1\n")
   348→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("failed to fetch metadata"))
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	// Now resume the session with the fetched metadata
   352→	return resumeSession(metadata.SessionID, checkpointID, false)
   353→}
   354→
   355→// resumeSession restores and displays the resume command for a specific session.
   356→// For multi-session checkpoints, restores ALL sessions and shows commands for each.
   357→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   358→func resumeSession(sessionID string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, force bool) error {
   359→	// Read checkpoint metadata first to get agent type (matching rewind pattern)
   360→	repo, err := openRepository()
   361→	if err != nil {
   362→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   363→	}
   364→
   365→	metadataTree, err := strategy.GetMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   366→	if err != nil {
   367→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch: %w", err)
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	metadata, err := strategy.ReadCheckpointMetadata(metadataTree, checkpointID.Path())
   371→	if err != nil {
   372→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Resolve agent from checkpoint metadata (same as rewind)
   376→	ag, err := strategy.ResolveAgentForRewind(metadata.Agent)
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve agent: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	// Initialize logging context with agent
   382→	ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "resume"), ag.Name())
   383→
   384→	logging.Debug(ctx, "resume session started",
   385→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   386→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
```

```
   429→// GetMetadataBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   430→func GetMetadataBranchTree(repo *git.Repository) (*object.Tree, error) {
   431→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   432→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   433→	if err != nil {
   434→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch reference: %w", err)

   440→	}
   441→
   442→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch tree: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→	return tree, nil
   447→}
```

```
   407→
   408→	repo, _, _ := setupResumeTestRepo(t, tmpDir, false)
```

```
+		return err

+	repo, err := OpenRepository()

+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
+	}

+	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
+		if _, err := InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
+			return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
```

All tests pass. Here's a summary of the fixes:

1. **config_test.go** - Removed `testSettingsAgent` const (used `"agent"` field removed from `EntireSettings`), replaced references with `testSettingsEnabled` or `{}`. Removed unused `strategy` import. Replaced `"strategy"` field in JSON with `"enabled"`.

2. **status_test.go** - Replaced all `{"strategy": "manual-commit", "enabled": true/false}` JSON strings with `{"enabled": true/false}`.

3. **settings/settings_test.go** - Replaced `"strategy": "manual-commit"` in test JSON with `"enabled": true`.

4. **integration_test/testenv_test.go** - Changed settings verification from checking for `"strategy"` field to checking for `"enabled"` field.

5. **resume_test.go** - Fixed trailing comma in JSON after the `"strategy"` line was removed (the comma after `"created_at"` made the JSON invalid).

```
    67→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates an E2E test environment ready for testing.
    68→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
    69→// checks out a feature branch, and sets up agent hooks.
    70→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
    71→	t.Helper()
    72→
    73→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    74→	env.InitRepo()
    75→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository\n\nThis is a test repository for E2E testing.\n")
    76→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    77→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    78→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/e2e-test")
    79→
    80→	// Use `entire enable` to set up everything (hooks, settings, etc.)
    81→	// This sets up .entire/settings.json and .claude/settings.json with hooks
    82→	env.RunEntireEnable(strategyName)
    83→
    84→	// Commit all files created by `entire enable` so they survive git stash -u operations.
    85→	// Without this, stash operations would stash away the hooks config and entire settings,
    86→	// causing hooks to not fire for subsequent prompts and stash pop conflicts.
    87→	// Using "git add ." is safe in test repos since we control all content.
    88→	env.GitAddAll()
    89→	env.GitCommit("Add entire and agent config")
    90→
    91→	return env
    92→}
    93→

   113→	}
```

```
    17→// RunEnableWithAccessibleMode runs `entire enable` without --strategy flag in accessible mode.
    18→// It provides stdin input to answer the telemetry prompt.
    19→func (env *TestEnv) RunEnableWithAccessibleMode() string {
    20→	env.T.Helper()
    21→
    22→	// Run CLI with ACCESSIBLE=1 for non-interactive prompts
    23→	// Provide "no" for telemetry
    24→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "enable")
    25→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    26→	cmd.Env = append(env.cliEnv(), "ACCESSIBLE=1")
    27→	// Provide input for telemetry prompt
    28→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader("no\n")
    29→
    30→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
    31→	if err != nil {
    32→		env.T.Fatalf("enable command failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    33→	}
    34→	return string(output)
    35→}
    36→
    37→// SetEnabled updates the enabled state in .entire/settings file
    38→func (env *TestEnv) SetEnabled(enabled bool) {
    39→	env.T.Helper()
    40→
    41→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", paths.SettingsFileName)
    42→
    43→	// Read existing settings
    44→	var settings map[string]interface{}
    45→	if data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath); err == nil {
    46→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
    47→			env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse settings: %v", err)
    48→		}
    49→	} else {
    50→		settings = make(map[string]interface{})
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	// Update enabled state
    54→	settings["enabled"] = enabled
    55→
    56→	// Write back
    57→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
    58→	if err != nil {
    59→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to marshal settings: %v", err)
    60→	}
    61→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
    62→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write settings: %v", err)
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→func TestEnableDisable(t *testing.T) {
    67→	t.Parallel()
    68→	RunForAllStrategiesWithBasicEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    69→		// Initially should be enabled (default)
    70→		stdout := env.RunCLI("status")
    71→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "Enabled") {
    72→			t.Errorf("Expected status to show 'Enabled', got: %s", stdout)
    73→		}
    74→
    75→		// Disable
    76→		stdout = env.RunCLI("disable")
    77→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "disabled") {
    78→			t.Errorf("Expected disable output to contain 'disabled', got: %s", stdout)
    79→		}
    80→
    81→		// Check status is now disabled
    82→		stdout = env.RunCLI("status")
    83→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "Disabled") {
    84→			t.Errorf("Expected status to show 'Disabled', got: %s", stdout)
    85→		}
    86→
    87→		// Re-enable (using --strategy flag for non-interactive mode)
    88→		stdout = env.RunCLI("enable", "--strategy", strategyName)
    89→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "Ready.") {
    90→			t.Errorf("Expected enable output to contain 'Ready.', got: %s", stdout)
    91→		}
    92→
    93→		// Check status is now enabled
    94→		stdout = env.RunCLI("status")
    95→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "Enabled") {
    96→			t.Errorf("Expected status to show 'Enabled', got: %s", stdout)
    97→		}
    98→	})
    99→}

   101→func TestRewindBlockedWhenDisabled(t *testing.T) {
   102→	t.Parallel()
   103→	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   104→		// Disable Entire
   105→		env.SetEnabled(false)
   106→
   107→		// Try to run rewind --list - should show disabled message (not error)
   108→		stdout, err := env.RunCLIWithError("rewind", "--list")
   109→		if err != nil {
   110→			t.Fatalf("rewind --list command failed unexpectedly: %v\nOutput: %s", err, stdout)
   111→		}
   112→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "Entire is disabled") {
   113→			t.Errorf("Expected disabled message, got: %s", stdout)
   114→		}
   115→		if !strings.Contains(stdout, "entire enable") {
   116→			t.Errorf("Expected message to mention 'entire enable', got: %s", stdout)
   117→		}
   118→	})
   119→}
   120→
   121→func TestHooksSilentWhenDisabled(t *testing.T) {
   122→	t.Parallel()
   123→	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   124→		// Create an untracked file
   125→		env.WriteFile("newfile.txt", "content")
   126→
   127→		// Disable Entire
   128→		env.SetEnabled(false)
   129→
   130→		// Run hook - should exit silently (no error, no state file created)
   131→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit("test-session-disabled")
   132→		if err != nil {
   133→			t.Fatalf("Hook should exit silently when disabled, got error: %v", err)
   134→		}
   135→
   136→		// Verify no state file was created (hook exited early)
   137→		statePath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp", "pre-prompt-test-session-disabled.json")
   138→		if _, err := os.Stat(statePath); err == nil {
   139→			t.Error("pre-prompt state file should NOT exist when disabled")
   140→		}
   141→	})
   142→}

   178→func TestEnableDefaultStrategy(t *testing.T) {
   179→	t.Parallel()
   180→
   181→	// Create a basic test environment with just a git repo (no Entire init)
   182→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   183→	env.InitRepo()
   184→

   191→	if !strings.Contains(stdout, "Ready.") {

   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Verify settings file has manual-commit strategy
   196→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", paths.SettingsFileName)

   200→	}
   201→
   202→	var settings map[string]interface{}

   205→	}
   206→
   207→	strategy, ok := settings["strategy"].(string)
   208→	if !ok {
   209→		t.Fatalf("Strategy not found in settings: %v", settings)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	if strategy != "manual-commit" {
   213→		t.Errorf("Expected default strategy to be 'manual-commit', got: %s", strategy)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Also verify via status command
   217→	stdout = env.RunCLI("status")
   218→	if !strings.Contains(stdout, "manual-commit") {
   219→		t.Errorf("Expected status to show 'manual-commit', got: %s", stdout)
   220→	}
```

```
   178→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates a TestEnv ready for session testing.
   179→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
   180→// and checks out a feature branch. This is the most common setup
   181→// for session and rewind tests since Entire tracking skips main/master.
   182→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
   183→	t.Helper()

   187→}

   189→// AllStrategies returns all strategy names for parameterized tests.
   190→func AllStrategies() []string {
   191→	return []string{
   192→		strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit,
   193→	}
   194→}
```

> AGENT

All the remaining failures are the pre-existing `TestResume_*` and `TestWorktreeCommitPersistence` tests that were already failing before our changes. The tests we broke (`TestEnableDisable`, `TestEnableWhenDisabled`, `TestEnableDefaultStrategy`, `TestTestEnv_InitEntire`, `TestStatusWhenDisabled`, `TestRewindBlockedWhenDisabled`, `TestHooksSilentWhenDisabled`) are now all passing.

Summary of fixes:
- **testenv.go** - Removed `"strategy"` and `"agent"` fields from the settings map written by `initEntireInternal`
- **setup_cmd_test.go** - Replaced `--strategy` flag with `--agent claude-code --telemetry=false` for non-interactive re-enable; changed `TestEnableDefaultStrategy` to verify `"enabled"` field instead of `"strategy"`; used `--project` on `disable` so re-enable can cleanly override
